## Protocols


<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `MCPClientTransport`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Protocols' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/client/_transports.py#L18' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Protocol for outbound MCP transports.

A transport is responsible for sending JSON-RPC requests to an external
MCP server and returning the parsed JSON-RPC response.  Each call maps
to one logical request-response pair; the transport handles any
framing details (length-delimited lines, HTTP bodies, etc.).

Protocol for handling MCP prompt-related methods.

Protocol for MCP transport implementations.

A transport handles the I/O layer — reading requests and
writing responses. The MCP server is transport-agnostic.

Abstract base class for MCP transport implementations.

A transport handles the I/O layer - reading requests and
writing responses. The MCP server is transport-agnostic.

### `ControllerPromptProvider`

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/controllers/_controller_providers.py#L164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
MCPPromptProviderProtocol backed by a list of MCPController instances.

Aggregates all ``@prompt``-decorated methods and dispatches
``get_prompt`` calls to the matching handler.

### `ControllerResourceProvider`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

MCPResourceProviderProtocol backed by a list of MCPController instances.

Aggregates all ``@resource``-decorated methods, exposes them for listing,
and routes ``read_resource`` calls via URI pattern matching.

MCPToolProviderProtocol backed by a list of MCPController instances.

Aggregates all ``@tool``-decorated methods across multiple controllers,
auto-generates JSON Schema from type annotations, and dispatches
``call_tool`` to the matching handler.

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `MCPClient`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/client/core.py#L80' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Client for connecting to and calling tools on external MCP servers.

Manages the MCP protocol handshake (``initialize`` / ``initialized``
notification) and exposes ergonomic methods for the most common MCP
operations.  The underlying <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcpclienttransport' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPClientTransport</a> handles the I/O;
see <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#stdioclienttransport' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>StdioClientTransport</a> and <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#sseclienttransport' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>SSEClientTransport</a>.

Supports async context manager usage for automatic lifecycle management

```python
async with MCPClient(transport) as client:
    tools = await client.list_tools()
```


    async with MCPClient(transport) as client:
        tools = await client.list_tools()

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `MCPClientModule`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/client/module.py#L357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Module for consuming external MCP servers via DI-injected clients.

Registers one or more <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcpclient' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPClient</a> instances (wrapped in an
<a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcpclientregistry' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPClientRegistry</a>) so any service in the application can call
external MCP tools.

Use configure to configure connections

```python
app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[
        MCPClientModule.configure(
            connections=[
                MCPConnection.stdio(
                    ["uvx", "mcp-server-git"],
                    name="git",
                ),
            ]
        ),
    ],
)
```


    app = LexigramApplication(
        modules=[
            MCPClientModule.configure(
                connections=[
                    MCPConnection.stdio(
                        ["uvx", "mcp-server-git"],
                        name="git",
                    ),
                ]
            ),
        ],
    )

Example

```python
MCPClientModule.configure(
    connections=[
        MCPConnection.stdio(["npx", "github-mcp"], name="github"),
        MCPConnection.sse("http://localhost:9000/mcp", name="local"),
    ],
)
```

        connections=[
            MCPConnection.stdio(["npx", "github-mcp"], name="github"),

        ],
    )

### `MCPClientProvider`

Registry of named <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcpclient' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPClient</a> instances.

Registered in the DI container by <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcpclientmodule' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPClientModule</a>.  Inject this
in services that need to call multiple external MCP servers

```python
class ReportService:
    def __init__(self, registry: MCPClientRegistry) -> None:
        self._git = registry.get("git")
        self._analytics = registry.get("analytics")
```


    class ReportService:
        def __init__(self, registry: MCPClientRegistry) -> None:
            self._git = registry.get("git")
            self._analytics = registry.get("analytics")

Call <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcpclient' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPClient</a>.connect before making requests (or use the client
as an async context manager for automatic lifecycle management).

Configuration for the MCP server.

Attributes:
    host: Host to bind to (for HTTP transport).
    port: Port to bind to (for HTTP transport).
    path: URL path for MCP endpoint (default /mcp).
    enable_sse: Enable Server-Sent Events for streaming responses.
    stdio_mode: Use stdio transport instead of HTTP.
    server_name: Name of the MCP server.
    server_version: Version of the MCP server.
    cors_origins: CORS allowed origins (for HTTP transport).

<hr style='border:none;border-top:1px solid rgba(200,255,0,0.2);margin:1.75rem 0 0 0;' />

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `MCPConnection`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/client/module.py#L78' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Immutable description of one external MCP server connection.

Do not construct directly — use the stdio or sse class
methods.

Attributes:
    name: Unique identifier for this connection used by
          <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcpclientregistry' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPClientRegistry</a>.
    transport_type: ``"stdio"`` or ``"sse"``.
    command: (stdio only) Subprocess command to launch the MCP server.
    url: (sse only) Base HTTP URL of the MCP server.
    env: (stdio only) Optional extra environment variables.
    headers: (sse only) Optional extra HTTP headers.
    request_timeout: Per-request timeout in seconds (default 30).
    startup_timeout: (stdio only) Wait timeout for process start (default 10).

<div style='padding-left:1rem;border-left:1px solid var(--sl-color-gray-5);margin-top:2rem;margin-bottom:2rem;'>

Create a connection that communicates with a stdio-based MCP server.

Spawns *command* as a subprocess and exchanges newline-delimited
JSON-RPC messages over its stdin/stdout.

Example

```python
MCPConnection.stdio(
    ["uvx", "mcp-server-git", "--repository", "/repo"],
    name="git",
)
```


    MCPConnection.stdio(
        ["uvx", "mcp-server-git", "--repository", "/repo"],
        name="git",
    )

Example

```python

    "http://analytics.internal/mcp",
    name="analytics",
    headers={"Authorization": "Bearer secret"},
)
```

        "http://analytics.internal/mcp",
        name="analytics",
        headers={"Authorization": "Bearer secret"},

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `MCPController`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/controllers/base.py#L10' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Base class for MCP controllers.

Groups related MCP tools, resources, and prompt templates into a single
class — mirroring the ``Controller`` DX from lexigram-web.  Register
decorated subclasses via ``MCPModule(controllers=[...])``.

Example

```python
class DataToolsController(MCPController):
    def __init__(self, repo: UserRepository) -> None:
        self.repo = repo

    @tool("get_user", description="Fetch a user by ID")
    async def get_user(self, user_id: str) -> dict:
        result = await self.repo.get(user_id)
        return result.unwrap_or({})
```


    class DataToolsController(MCPController):
        def __init__(self, repo: UserRepository) -> None:
            self.repo = repo

        @tool("get_user", description="Fetch a user by ID")
        async def get_user(self, user_id: str) -> dict:
            result = await self.repo.get(user_id)
            return result.unwrap_or({})

### `MCPModule`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/module.py#L14' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Module for MCP server.

Provides MCP server, handlers, and transports to the application.

Usage

```python
app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[..., MCPModule.configure()],
)

# With MCPController subclasses::

app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[..., MCPModule.configure(controllers=[DataToolsController])],
)

# Auto-expose existing services as MCP tools::

app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[
        ...,
        MCPModule.from_services(
            services=[UserService, AnalyticsService],
            include_methods=["search", "get_*"],
        ),
    ],
)
```


    app = LexigramApplication(
        modules=[..., MCPModule.configure()],
    )

    # With MCPController subclasses

```python
app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[..., MCPModule.configure(controllers=[DataToolsController])],
)

# Auto-expose existing services as MCP tools::

app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[
        ...,
        MCPModule.from_services(
            services=[UserService, AnalyticsService],
            include_methods=["search", "get_*"],
        ),
    ],
)
```


    app = LexigramApplication(
        modules=[..., MCPModule.configure(controllers=[DataToolsController])],
    )

    # Auto-expose existing services as MCP tools

```python
app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[
        ...,
        MCPModule.from_services(
            services=[UserService, AnalyticsService],
            include_methods=["search", "get_*"],
        ),
    ],
)
```


    app = LexigramApplication(
        modules=[
            ...,
            MCPModule.from_services(
                services=[UserService, AnalyticsService],
                include_methods=["search", "get_*"],
            ),
        ],
    )

Create an MCPModule suitable for unit and integration testing.

Uses in-memory transport with no external MCP server connections.
Streaming is disabled by default to simplify test assertions.

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `MCPServer`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/server/core.py#L22' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Core MCP server — routes JSON-RPC messages to handlers.

The server is transport-agnostic. It receives parsed JSON-RPC
messages and returns JSON-RPC responses. The transport layer
(stdio or HTTP+SSE) handles serialization and I/O.

Usage

```python
server = MCPServer(
    name="my-app",
    tool_handler=tool_handler,
    resource_handler=resource_handler,
)
response = await server.handle_message(request)
```


    server = MCPServer(
        name="my-app",
        tool_handler=tool_handler,
        resource_handler=resource_handler,
    )
    response = await server.handle_message(request)

Tool provider built from module-level ``@tool``-decorated functions.

Use from_module to scan a Python module for decorated callables

```python
import my_tools
provider = ModuleToolProvider.from_module(my_tools)
```


    import my_tools
    provider = ModuleToolProvider.from_module(my_tools)

Or pass functions directly

```python
provider = ModuleToolProvider([search_users, create_user])
```


    provider = ModuleToolProvider([search_users, create_user])

Handler for MCP prompt-related methods.

Handles prompts/list and prompts/get methods by delegating to an
MCPPromptProviderProtocol implementation.

Handler for MCP resource-related methods.

Handles resources/list, resources/read, and resources/templates/list
methods by delegating to an MCPResourceProviderProtocol implementation.

MCP client transport that communicates with an HTTP+SSE MCP server.

Sends JSON-RPC requests as HTTP POST bodies and reads the responses from
the response body.  Uses ``aiohttp`` for async HTTP — ensure it is
installed (``pip install aiohttp`` or the ``http`` extra of this package).

Server-Sent Events transport for MCP server.

This transport uses HTTP POST for requests and SSE for streaming
responses. Ideal for web-based integrations.

Receive is not applicable for SSE (pull-based).

For HTTP+SSE, use the HTTP endpoint directly instead.

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `ServiceToolProvider`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/controllers/_service_providers.py#L11' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Tool provider that auto-exposes public methods of service instances as MCP tools.

Created via <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcpmodule' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPModule</a>.from_services or directly

```python
provider = ServiceToolProvider.from_services(
    [user_service, analytics_service],
    include_patterns=["search", "get_*"],
)
```


    provider = ServiceToolProvider.from_services(
        [user_service, analytics_service],
        include_patterns=["search", "get_*"],
    )

Tool names are prefixed with the lowercase service class name to avoid
collisions (e.g. ``UserService.search_users()`` → ``"userservice_search_users"``).
The first line of each method's docstring is used as the tool description.
Both ``async def`` and plain ``def`` methods are supported.

<div style='padding-left:1rem;border-left:1px solid var(--sl-color-gray-5);margin-top:2rem;margin-bottom:2rem;'>

### `StdioClientTransport`

MCP client transport that communicates with a subprocess via stdio.

Spawns *command* as an asyncio subprocess and exchanges newline-delimited
JSON-RPC messages over its stdin/stdout pipes.

Stdio-based transport for MCP server.

This transport reads JSON-RPC messages from stdin and writes
responses to stdout. Ideal for CLI/desktop integration.

<div style='padding-left:1rem;border-left:1px solid var(--sl-color-gray-5);margin-top:2rem;margin-bottom:2rem;'>

Handler for MCP tool-related methods.

Handles tools/list and tools/call methods by delegating to a
MCPToolProviderProtocol implementation.

All handler methods return ``Result`` so callers can handle expected
failures without raising exceptions.

<div style='padding-left:1rem;border-left:1px solid var(--sl-color-gray-5);margin-top:2rem;margin-bottom:2rem;'>

Handle tools/call method.

Returns a ``Result`` — ``Ok`` wraps the serialized MCP tool result dict;
``Err`` wraps an <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-mcp/api/#mcptoolcallerror' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>MCPToolCallError</a>
describing what went wrong.

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `ToolRegistryAdapter`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-mcp/src/lexigram/ai/mcp/adapters/tool_adapter.py#L21' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Adapter that bridges lexigram-agents ToolRegistry to MCPToolProviderProtocol.

This adapter allows the MCP server to use the @tool-decorated
functions from lexigram-agents.  The translation is minimal:

* ``ToolProtocol.parameters_schema`` → MCP ``inputSchema``
* ``ToolRegistryProtocol.get()``     → lookup by name
* ``ToolProtocol.execute(**kw)``     → tool invocation

```python

    return "Summarize the following data..."
```

collect_resources.

Protocol violation (malformed message, invalid state).

Raised when an MCP message violates the protocol specification,
such as missing required fields or invalid JSON-RPC structure.

when a resource is not found or cannot be read.

the tool not existing or raising an exception.
